Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the giants object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 937. The moduli space has dimension at most 893 with 4 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the giants space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 937 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 893. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The giants variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 832 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 60 and intersection form of rank 3.
